| Len | Hey, it's Akito and Toya!<br>Are you going to MEIKO's cafe? | talk |
| Akito | Nah, we've got a gig tonight, so we came over to kill some time and rehearse. | talk |
| Len | Oh, I was just about to start practice too! | talk |
| Len | I know a good place. Want to come along? | talk |
| Akito | Sure, why not. Where is it? | talk |
| Len | It's my special secret spot for practicing! I normally keep it to myself, but I don't mind showing you. | talk |
| Akito | Fine, I'll bite.<br>Toya, you okay with going with Len? | talk |
| Toya | Yeah, sure... | talk |
| Len | Alright!<br>Follow me! | talk |
| Len | By the way, isn't there something you want to tell me? | talk |
| Akito | Huh? Tell you about what...? | talk |
| Len | You know, isn't there something on your mind?<br>Something you want to talk about? | talk |
| Toya | No, not really... | talk |
| Akito | Hey, stop beating around the bush and just tell us. | talk |
| Len | Oh, come on! I mean Kohane and An, obviously! | talk |
| Akito | Oh, that. | talk |
| Len | You can always talk to me when you're having problems with your partners! | talk |
| Toya | Oh, okay.<br>You wanted us to do that. | talk |
| Akito | But I knew that they'd sort things out by themselves, so I wasn't really worried. | talk |
| Len | Hmm. Really? | talk |
| Len | You know, Miku said almost the exact same thing. | talk |
| Akito | Really? What did she say? | talk |
| Len | “They'll sort it out between themselves.”<br>She pretended not to notice what was going on. | talk |
| Akito | Oh? | talk |
| Len | I think that part of her is really cool. | talk |
| Len | But I'm not like that. If someone's having a rough time, I want to lend them a hand. | talk |
| Toya | We're the same then... | talk |
| Toya | My friends are always helping me out.<br>I really want to do something for them too. | talk |
| Len | Yeah, that's exactly how I feel. | talk |
| Toya | I owe Akito the most, so I'm always thinking of ways to help or support him... | talk |
| Toya | But he's got it all together, so I never get any opportunities to do that. | talk |
| Akito | Hm...? | talk |
| Toya | On one hand, it's great that he's so independent, but a little frustrating at the same time. | talk |
| Len | Hmm, I think I see what you mean...? | talk |
| Akito | Toya, you really didn't know...? | talk |
| Akito | I rely on you a lot. | talk |
| Toya | You do...? | talk |
| Akito | Sure. There were a lot of things I couldn't do before we teamed up. | talk |
| Akito | When I was by myself, I just glossed over so many things and tried to just force my way over each hurdle. | talk |
| Akito | But your musical talent made me realize that it wasn't good enough. | talk |
| Toya | I see...<br>So I did help you improve in some way. | talk |
| Akito | If you didn't, we wouldn't still be partners. | talk |
| Len | I noticed this about you too, Akito...<br>You're surprisingly frank or earnest sometimes. | talk |
| Akito | What are you talking about?<br>I'm just speaking my mind. | talk |
| Akito | Anyway, we've been walking a while.<br>We not there yet? How much longer is it? | talk |
| Len | Huh? | talk |
| Len | Oh no! We already passed it by! | talk |
| Akito | Dude...<br>Get it together... | talk |
| Len | I'm really sorry! You've got a gig tonight and I ended up wasting your time... Let's backtrack a bit! | talk |
| Toya | There's no rush.<br>We still have plenty of time. | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, it's no big deal.<br>We can stick around a while longer. | talk |

| Akito | I mean, RAD WEEKEND was nuts from start to finish, right? | talk |

| Soccer Team Member | Hey, long time no see!<br>You too, Toya, Shiraishi. Welcome back. | talk |
| An | Hey, thanks! | talk |
| Akito | Hey there. Club today? | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Yeah, just got back from my hill sprint. The others should be back soon too... | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Oh, Akito, now that you're back, would you mind lending us a hand? | talk |
| Akito | Huh? Oh... | talk |
| Akito | Sorry, I'll pass.<br>I have to go through my daily training routine after this. | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | What? But it's been so long. You're no fun. Still, if you have stuff to do, then there's not much we can do about that... | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | More importantly, you guys went over to the United States, right? Anything cool happen? | talk |
| Akito | That's a pretty broad question. | talk |
| Akito | I mean, we ran into our fair share of trouble.<br>We couldn't stay at the hotel we thought we had booked, and I had my wallet stolen. | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Haha. That's terrible. But sounds like you had fun! | talk |
| Akito | How...?<br>Still, all that stuff aside, it actually was fun. | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | That's pretty cool. I still can't believe that you took a trip overseas. I mean, you had to speak in English, right? | talk |
| Akito | Fortunately, I had Toya and a few others there to help me out. Some of the people we met could even speak Japanese. | talk |
| Toya | But you did just fine by yourself when you bought things at the airport and elsewhere. | talk |
| Akito | I mean, that was just me stringing together simple sentences... | talk |
| Akito | Considering what we wanna do in the future, I need to buckle down and get better at it. | talk |
| An | Yeah, we gotta keep doing our best there. | talk |
| An | Also, after spending a day at school, I feel like I should cherish the time I get to spend here more... | talk |
| Toya | Yes, I thought that as well. | talk |
| Toya | We can just talk without really working at it, and people will understand where we're coming from... | talk |
| Toya | Thinking about it that way, it's rather reassuring, isn't it? | talk |
| Akito | I more or less get what you're trying to say... | talk |
| Akito | (I also felt that a little after coming to school for the first time in a while.) | talk |
| Akito | (I'd taken that for granted until now, but it's something that I really shouldn't...) | talk |
| An | Then, why don't you make an exception today? | talk |
| Akito | Huh? | talk |
| Toya | Why don't you go and help the soccer team? | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | What...?! | talk |
| Akito | Wait, how did you guys land on that...? | talk |
| Toya | It's a part of us cherishing our time here.<br>Also, it's important to take your mind off of things every once in a while. | talk |
| Akito | (Yeah, I guess every once in a while...) | talk |
| Akito | When the others get back, are you guys gonna have a quick match? | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Y-Yeah, that's right...<br>Does this mean you're gonna join us?! | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, for a little bit. | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Thanks! You're the best! | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Try to get warmed up before the others get back! | talk |
| Akito | Okay, I will. | talk |
| An | In that case, Toya, we should go watch them! | talk |
| Toya | Yes, let's do that. | talk |
| Akito | You two aren't going home?<br>Don't come crying to me if the ball goes flying towards you guys. | talk |
| Toya | I'll do my best to dodge it if it does... | talk |
| An | Haha! You'll be fine! I'll just kick it away! | talk |
| Akito | Oh? I'd like to see that. | talk |
| Soccer Team Member | Alright, Akito, I'll help you get warmed up! | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, you better. I'm expecting at least that much of you. | talk |
| Akito | (I guess doing stuff like this every once in a while isn't so bad...) | talk |
| Akito | Anyway, I'll go and start getting ready. | talk |
